----- FILE: backups\message-notifications-20261017-070724\page.tsx -----
import Link from "next/link";
import { prisma } from "@/src/lib/prisma";
import { getCurrentUser } from "@/src/lib/auth";
import ReplyForm from "./ReplyForm";
import "./messages.css";

export const instant = false;

type ConversationItem = {
  id: string;
  listingId: string;
  senderId: string;
  recipientId: string;
  message: string;
  createdAt: Date;
  isRead: boolean;
  listing: {
    id: string;
    title: string;
  };
  sender: {
    name: string | null;
  };
  recipient: {
    name: string | null;
  };
};

type Conversation = {
  key: string;
  listingId: string;
  listingTitle: string;
  counterpartId: string;
  counterpartName: string;
  messages: ConversationItem[];
};

function dateLabel(date: Date) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fa-IR", {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

export default async function MessagesPage() {
  const user = await getCurrentUser();

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <main className="hm-page" dir="rtl">
        <div className="hm-container hm-empty">
          <h1>گفت‌وگوهای خانه‌یار</h1>
          <p>برای دیدن مکالمات، ابتدا وارد حساب شوید.</p>
          <Link
            href="/login?next=%2Fmy%2Fmessages"
            className="hm-button"
          >
            ورود به حساب
          </Link>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const records = await prisma.inquiry.findMany({
    where: {
      OR: [
        { senderId: user.id },
        { recipientId: user.id },
      ],
    },
    orderBy: [
      { createdAt: "desc" },
      { id: "desc" },
    ],
    take: 200,
    select: {
      id: true,
      listingId: true,
      senderId: true,
      recipientId: true,
      message: true,
      createdAt: true,
      isRead: true,
      listing: {
        select: { id: true, title: true },
      },
      sender: {
        select: { name: true },
      },
      recipient: {
        select: { name: true },
      },
    },
  });

  const conversations = new Map<string, Conversation>();

  for (const item of records) {
    const outgoing = item.senderId === user.id;

    const counterpartId = outgoing
      ? item.recipientId
      : item.senderId;

    const counterpartName = outgoing
      ? item.recipient.name || "کاربر خانه‌یار"
      : item.sender.name || "کاربر خانه‌یار";

    const key = `${item.listingId}:${counterpartId}`;
    let conversation = conversations.get(key);

    if (!conversation) {
      conversation = {
        key,
        listingId: item.listingId,
        listingTitle: item.listing.title,
        counterpartId,
        counterpartName,
        messages: [],
      };

      conversations.set(key, conversation);
    }

    conversation.messages.push(item);
  }

  const threads = Array.from(conversations.values());

  for (const thread of threads) {
    thread.messages.reverse();
  }

  return (
    <main className="hm-page" dir="rtl">
      <header className="hm-header">
        <div className="hm-container">
          <Link href="/" className="hm-brand">
            ⌂ خانه‌یار
          </Link>
          <h1>گفت‌وگوهای من</h1>
          <p>
            پیام‌ها و پاسخ‌های مربوط به هر ملک، یکجا.
          </p>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="hm-container hm-content">
        <nav className="hm-navigation" aria-label="پنل کاربری">
          <Link href="/dashboard/listings">آگهی‌های من</Link>
          <Link href="/favorites">علاقه‌مندی‌ها</Link>
          <Link href="/search">جست‌وجوی ملک</Link>
        </nav>

        <div className="hm-section-head">
          <h2>مکالمات</h2>
          <span>{threads.length.toLocaleString("fa-IR")}</span>
        </div>

        {threads.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="hm-empty">
            <h2>هنوز مکالمه‌ای نداری</h2>
            <p>
              برای شروع، وارد صفحه یک آگهی شو و به مالک پیام بده.
            </p>
            <Link href="/search" className="hm-button">
              جست‌وجوی ملک
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="hm-thread-list">
            {threads.map((thread) => {
              const latest = thread.messages.at(-1);

              return (
                <section key={thread.key} className="hm-thread">
                  <div className="hm-thread-header">
                    <div>
                      <span className="hm-thread-kicker">
                        مکالمه درباره ملک
                      </span>

                      <h2>{thread.listingTitle}</h2>

                      <p>
                        طرف گفت‌وگو: {thread.counterpartName}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <Link
                      href={`/listings/${encodeURIComponent(
                        thread.listingId
                      )}`}
                      className="hm-listing-link"
                    >
                      مشاهده آگهی ←
                    </Link>
                  </div>

                  <div className="hm-thread-messages">
                    {thread.messages.map((item) => {
                      const own = item.senderId === user.id;

                      return (
                        <article
                          key={item.id}
                          className={
                            own
                              ? "hm-bubble hm-bubble-own"
                              : "hm-bubble hm-bubble-other"
                          }
                        >
                          <span className="hm-bubble-author">
                            {own ? "شما" : thread.counterpartName}
                          </span>

                          <p>{item.message}</p>

                          <time dateTime={item.createdAt.toISOString()}>
                            {dateLabel(item.createdAt)}
                          </time>
                        </article>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>

                  {latest && latest.recipientId === user.id ? (
                    <ReplyForm replyToId={latest.id} />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="hm-awaiting">
                      آخرین پیام را شما ارسال کرده‌اید؛
                      برای پاسخ طرف مقابل منتظر بمانید.
                    </div>
                  )}
                </section>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        <p className="hm-limit-note">
          در این نسخه حداکثر ۲۰۰ پیام اخیر بارگذاری می‌شود.
          گفت‌وگوهای طولانی‌تر در مرحله صفحه‌بندی تکمیل خواهند شد.
        </p>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { prisma } from "@/src/lib/prisma";
import { getCurrentUser } from "@/src/lib/auth";
import ReplyForm from "./ReplyForm";
import MarkReadButton from "./MarkReadButton";
import "./messages.css";

export const instant = false;

type ConversationItem = {
  id: string;
  listingId: string;
  senderId: string;
  recipientId: string;
  message: string;
  createdAt: Date;
  isRead: boolean;
  listing: {
    id: string;
    title: string;
  };
  sender: {
    name: string | null;
  };
  recipient: {
    name: string | null;
  };
};

type Conversation = {
  key: string;
  listingId: string;
  listingTitle: string;
  counterpartId: string;
  counterpartName: string;
  messages: ConversationItem[];
};

function dateLabel(date: Date) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fa-IR", {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

export default async function MessagesPage() {
  const user = await getCurrentUser();

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <main className="hm-page" dir="rtl">
        <div className="hm-container hm-empty">
          <h1>گفت‌وگوهای خانه‌یار</h1>
          <p>برای دیدن مکالمات، ابتدا وارد حساب شوید.</p>
          <Link
            href="/login?next=%2Fmy%2Fmessages"
            className="hm-button"
          >
            ورود به حساب
          </Link>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const records = await prisma.inquiry.findMany({
    where: {
      OR: [
        { senderId: user.id },
        { recipientId: user.id },
      ],
    },
    orderBy: [
      { createdAt: "desc" },
      { id: "desc" },
    ],
    take: 200,
    select: {
      id: true,
      listingId: true,
      senderId: true,
      recipientId: true,
      message: true,
      createdAt: true,
      isRead: true,
      listing: {
        select: { id: true, title: true },
      },
      sender: {
        select: { name: true },
      },
      recipient: {
        select: { name: true },
      },
    },
  });

  const conversations = new Map<string, Conversation>();

  for (const item of records) {
    const outgoing = item.senderId === user.id;

    const counterpartId = outgoing
      ? item.recipientId
      : item.senderId;

    const counterpartName = outgoing
      ? item.recipient.name || "کاربر خانه‌یار"
      : item.sender.name || "کاربر خانه‌یار";

    const key = `${item.listingId}:${counterpartId}`;
    let conversation = conversations.get(key);

    if (!conversation) {
      conversation = {
        key,
        listingId: item.listingId,
        listingTitle: item.listing.title,
        counterpartId,
        counterpartName,
        messages: [],
      };

      conversations.set(key, conversation);
    }

    conversation.messages.push(item);
  }

  const totalUnread = await prisma.inquiry.count({
    where: {
      recipientId: user.id,
      isRead: false,
    },
  });

  const threads = Array.from(conversations.values());

  for (const thread of threads) {
    thread.messages.reverse();
  }

  return (
    <main className="hm-page" dir="rtl">
      <header className="hm-header">
        <div className="hm-container">
          <Link href="/" className="hm-brand">
            ⌂ خانه‌یار
          </Link>
          <h1>گفت‌وگوهای من</h1>
          <div className="hm-global-unread" role="status">
            {totalUnread === 0
              ? "همه پیام‌های دریافتی خوانده شده‌اند."
              : `${totalUnread.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} پیام خوانده‌نشده`}
          </div>
          <p>
            پیام‌ها و پاسخ‌های مربوط به هر ملک، یکجا.
          </p>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="hm-container hm-content">
        <nav className="hm-navigation" aria-label="پنل کاربری">
          <Link href="/dashboard/listings">آگهی‌های من</Link>
          <Link href="/favorites">علاقه‌مندی‌ها</Link>
          <Link href="/search">جست‌وجوی ملک</Link>
        </nav>

        <div className="hm-section-head">
          <h2>مکالمات</h2>
          <span>{threads.length.toLocaleString("fa-IR")}</span>
        </div>

        {threads.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="hm-empty">
            <h2>هنوز مکالمه‌ای نداری</h2>
            <p>
              برای شروع، وارد صفحه یک آگهی شو و به مالک پیام بده.
            </p>
            <Link href="/search" className="hm-button">
              جست‌وجوی ملک
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="hm-thread-list">
            {threads.map((thread) => {
              const latest = thread.messages.at(-1);

              const unreadCount = thread.messages.filter(
                (message) =>
                  message.recipientId === user.id &&
                  !message.isRead
              ).length;

              return (
                <section key={thread.key} className="hm-thread">
                  <div className="hm-thread-header">
                    <div>
                      <span className="hm-thread-kicker">
                        مکالمه درباره ملک
                      </span>

                      <h2>{thread.listingTitle}</h2>

                      <p>
                        طرف گفت‌وگو: {thread.counterpartName}
                      </p>

                      {unreadCount > 0 && (
                        <span className="hm-thread-unread">
                          {unreadCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} پیام جدید
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <Link
                      href={`/listings/${encodeURIComponent(
                        thread.listingId
                      )}`}
                      className="hm-listing-link"
                    >
                      مشاهده آگهی ←
                    </Link>
                  </div>

                  <div className="hm-thread-messages">
                    {thread.messages.map((item) => {
                      const own = item.senderId === user.id;

                      return (
                        <article
                          key={item.id}
                          className={
                            own
                              ? "hm-bubble hm-bubble-own"
                              : "hm-bubble hm-bubble-other"
                          }
                        >
                          <span className="hm-bubble-author">
                            {own ? "شما" : thread.counterpartName}
                          </span>

                          <p>{item.message}</p>

                          <time dateTime={item.createdAt.toISOString()}>
                            {dateLabel(item.createdAt)}
                          </time>
                        </article>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>

                  <MarkReadButton
                    listingId={thread.listingId}
                    counterpartId={thread.counterpartId}
                    unreadCount={unreadCount}
                  />

                  {latest && latest.recipientId === user.id ? (
                    <ReplyForm replyToId={latest.id} />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="hm-awaiting">
                      آخرین پیام را شما ارسال کرده‌اید؛
                      برای پاسخ طرف مقابل منتظر بمانید.
                    </div>
                  )}
                </section>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        <p className="hm-limit-note">
          در این نسخه حداکثر ۲۰۰ پیام اخیر بارگذاری می‌شود.
          گفت‌وگوهای طولانی‌تر در مرحله صفحه‌بندی تکمیل خواهند شد.
        </p>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { prisma } from "@/src/lib/prisma";
import { getCurrentUser } from "@/src/lib/auth";
import ReplyForm from "./ReplyForm";
import MarkReadButton from "./MarkReadButton";
import "./messages.css";

export const instant = false;

type ConversationItem = {
  id: string;
  listingId: string;
  senderId: string;
  recipientId: string;
  message: string;
  createdAt: Date;
  isRead: boolean;
  listing: {
    id: string;
    title: string;
  };
  sender: {
    name: string | null;
  };
  recipient: {
    name: string | null;
  };
};

type Conversation = {
  key: string;
  listingId: string;
  listingTitle: string;
  counterpartId: string;
  counterpartName: string;
  messages: ConversationItem[];
};

function dateLabel(date: Date) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fa-IR", {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

export default async function MessagesPage() {
  const user = await getCurrentUser();

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <main className="hm-page" dir="rtl">
        <div className="hm-container hm-empty">
          <h1>گفت‌وگوهای خانه‌یار</h1>
          <p>برای دیدن مکالمات، ابتدا وارد حساب شوید.</p>
          <Link
            href="/login?next=%2Fmy%2Fmessages"
            className="hm-button"
          >
            ورود به حساب
          </Link>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const records = await prisma.inquiry.findMany({
    where: {
      OR: [
        { senderId: user.id },
        { recipientId: user.id },
      ],
    },
    orderBy: [
      { createdAt: "desc" },
      { id: "desc" },
    ],
    take: 200,
    select: {
      id: true,
      listingId: true,
      senderId: true,
      recipientId: true,
      message: true,
      createdAt: true,
      isRead: true,
      listing: {
        select: { id: true, title: true },
      },
      sender: {
        select: { name: true },
      },
      recipient: {
        select: { name: true },
      },
    },
  });

  const conversations = new Map<string, Conversation>();

  for (const item of records) {
    const outgoing = item.senderId === user.id;

    const counterpartId = outgoing
      ? item.recipientId
      : item.senderId;

    const counterpartName = outgoing
      ? item.recipient.name || "کاربر خانه‌یار"
      : item.sender.name || "کاربر خانه‌یار";

    const key = `${item.listingId}:${counterpartId}`;
    let conversation = conversations.get(key);

    if (!conversation) {
      conversation = {
        key,
        listingId: item.listingId,
        listingTitle: item.listing.title,
        counterpartId,
        counterpartName,
        messages: [],
      };

      conversations.set(key, conversation);
    }

    conversation.messages.push(item);
  }

  const totalUnread = await prisma.inquiry.count({
    where: {
      recipientId: user.id,
      isRead: false,
    },
  });

  const threads = Array.from(conversations.values());

  for (const thread of threads) {
    thread.messages.reverse();
  }

  return (
    <main className="hm-page" dir="rtl">


      <div className="hm-container hm-content">


        <div className="hm-section-head">
          <h2>مکالمات</h2>
          <span>{threads.length.toLocaleString("fa-IR")}</span>
        </div>

        {threads.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="hm-empty">
            <h2>هنوز مکالمه‌ای نداری</h2>
            <p>
              برای شروع، وارد صفحه یک آگهی شو و به مالک پیام بده.
            </p>
            <Link href="/search" className="hm-button">
              جست‌وجوی ملک
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="hm-thread-list">
            {threads.map((thread) => {
              const latest = thread.messages.at(-1);

              const unreadCount = thread.messages.filter(
                (message) =>
                  message.recipientId === user.id &&
                  !message.isRead
              ).length;

              return (
                <section key={thread.key} className="hm-thread">
                  <div className="hm-thread-header">
                    <div>
                      <span className="hm-thread-kicker">
                        مکالمه درباره ملک
                      </span>

                      <h2>{thread.listingTitle}</h2>

                      <p>
                        طرف گفت‌وگو: {thread.counterpartName}
                      </p>

                      {unreadCount > 0 && (
                        <span className="hm-thread-unread">
                          {unreadCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} پیام جدید
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <Link
                      href={`/listings/${encodeURIComponent(
                        thread.listingId
                      )}`}
                      className="hm-listing-link"
                    >
                      مشاهده آگهی ←
                    </Link>
                  </div>

                  <div className="hm-thread-messages">
                    {thread.messages.map((item) => {
                      const own = item.senderId === user.id;

                      return (
                        <article
                          key={item.id}
                          className={
                            own
                              ? "hm-bubble hm-bubble-own"
                              : "hm-bubble hm-bubble-other"
                          }
                        >
                          <span className="hm-bubble-author">
                            {own ? "شما" : thread.counterpartName}
                          </span>

                          <p>{item.message}</p>

                          <time dateTime={item.createdAt.toISOString()}>
                            {dateLabel(item.createdAt)}
                          </time>
                        </article>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>

                  <MarkReadButton
                    listingId={thread.listingId}
                    counterpartId={thread.counterpartId}
                    unreadCount={unreadCount}
                  />

                  {latest && latest.recipientId === user.id ? (
                    <ReplyForm replyToId={latest.id} />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="hm-awaiting">
                      آخرین پیام را شما ارسال کرده‌اید؛
                      برای پاسخ طرف مقابل منتظر بمانید.
                    </div>
                  )}
                </section>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        <p className="hm-limit-note">
          در این نسخه حداکثر ۲۰۰ پیام اخیر بارگذاری می‌شود.
          گفت‌وگوهای طولانی‌تر در مرحله صفحه‌بندی تکمیل خواهند شد.
        </p>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";
import { prisma } from "@/src/lib/prisma";
import { getCurrentUser } from "@/src/lib/auth";
import ReplyForm from "./ReplyForm";
import MarkReadButton from "./MarkReadButton";
import "./messages.css";

export const instant = false;

type ConversationItem = {
  id: string;
  listingId: string;
  senderId: string;
  recipientId: string;
  message: string;
  createdAt: Date;
  isRead: boolean;
  listing: {
    id: string;
    title: string;
  };
  sender: {
    name: string | null;
  };
  recipient: {
    name: string | null;
  };
};

type Conversation = {
  key: string;
  listingId: string;
  listingTitle: string;
  counterpartId: string;
  counterpartName: string;
  messages: ConversationItem[];
};

function dateLabel(date: Date) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fa-IR", {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

export default async function MessagesPage() {
  const user = await getCurrentUser();

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <main className="hm-page" dir="rtl">
        <div className="hm-container hm-empty">
          <h1>گفت‌وگوهای خانه‌یار</h1>
          <p>برای دیدن مکالمات، ابتدا وارد حساب شوید.</p>
          <Link
            href="/login?next=%2Fmy%2Fmessages"
            className="hm-button"
          >
            ورود به حساب
          </Link>
        </div>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const records = await prisma.inquiry.findMany({
    where: {
      OR: [
        { senderId: user.id },
        { recipientId: user.id },
      ],
    },
    orderBy: [
      { createdAt: "desc" },
      { id: "desc" },
    ],
    take: 200,
    select: {
      id: true,
      listingId: true,
      senderId: true,
      recipientId: true,
      message: true,
      createdAt: true,
      isRead: true,
      listing: {
        select: { id: true, title: true },
      },
      sender: {
        select: { name: true },
      },
      recipient: {
        select: { name: true },
      },
    },
  });

  const conversations = new Map<string, Conversation>();

  for (const item of records) {
    const outgoing = item.senderId === user.id;

    const counterpartId = outgoing
      ? item.recipientId
      : item.senderId;

    const counterpartName = outgoing
      ? item.recipient.name || "کاربر خانه‌یار"
      : item.sender.name || "کاربر خانه‌یار";

    const key = `${item.listingId}:${counterpartId}`;
    let conversation = conversations.get(key);

    if (!conversation) {
      conversation = {
        key,
        listingId: item.listingId,
        listingTitle: item.listing.title,
        counterpartId,
        counterpartName,
        messages: [],
      };

      conversations.set(key, conversation);
    }

    conversation.messages.push(item);
  }

  const totalUnread = await prisma.inquiry.count({
    where: {
      recipientId: user.id,
      isRead: false,
    },
  });

  const threads = Array.from(conversations.values());

  for (const thread of threads) {
    thread.messages.reverse();
  }

  return (
    <main className="hm-page" dir="rtl">


      <div className="hm-container hm-content">


        <div className="hm-section-head">
          <h2>مکالمات</h2>
          <span>{threads.length.toLocaleString("fa-IR")}</span>
        </div>

        {threads.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="hm-empty">
            <h2>هنوز مکالمه‌ای نداری</h2>
            <p>
              برای شروع، وارد صفحه یک آگهی شو و به مالک پیام بده.
            </p>
            <Link href="/search" className="hm-button">
              جست‌وجوی ملک
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="hm-thread-list">
            {threads.map((thread) => {
              const latest = thread.messages.at(-1);

              const unreadCount = thread.messages.filter(
                (message) =>
                  message.recipientId === user.id &&
                  !message.isRead
              ).length;

              return (
                <section key={thread.key} className="hm-thread">
                  <div className="hm-thread-header">
                    <div>
                      <span className="hm-thread-kicker">
                        مکالمه درباره ملک
                      </span>

                      <h2>{thread.listingTitle}</h2>

                      <p>
                        طرف گفت‌وگو: {thread.counterpartName}
                      </p>

                      {unreadCount > 0 && (
                        <span className="hm-thread-unread">
                          {unreadCount.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} پیام جدید
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    <Link
                      href={`/listings/${encodeURIComponent(
                        thread.listingId
                      )}`}
                      className="hm-listing-link"
                    >
                      مشاهده آگهی ←
                    </Link>
                  </div>

                  <div className="hm-thread-messages">
                    {thread.messages.map((item) => {
                      const own = item.senderId === user.id;

                      return (
                        <article
                          key={item.id}
                          className={
                            own
                              ? "hm-bubble hm-bubble-own"
                              : "hm-bubble hm-bubble-other"
                          }
                        >
                          <span className="hm-bubble-author">
                            {own ? "شما" : thread.counterpartName}
                          </span>

                          <p>{item.message}</p>

                          <time dateTime={item.createdAt.toISOString()}>
                            {dateLabel(item.createdAt)}
                          </time>
                        </article>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>

                  <MarkReadButton
                    listingId={thread.listingId}
                    counterpartId={thread.counterpartId}
                    unreadCount={unreadCount}
                  />

                  {latest && latest.recipientId === user.id ? (
                    <ReplyForm replyToId={latest.id} />
                  ) : (
                    <div className="hm-awaiting">
                      آخرین پیام را شما ارسال کرده‌اید؛
                      برای پاسخ طرف مقابل منتظر بمانید.
                    </div>
                  )}
                </section>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        <p className="hm-limit-note">
          در این نسخه حداکثر ۲۰۰ پیام اخیر بارگذاری می‌شود.
          گفت‌وگوهای طولانی‌تر در مرحله صفحه‌بندی تکمیل خواهند شد.
        </p>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

----- FILE: backups\my-listings-style-20261018-023753\page.tsx -----
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";

type Listing = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  city: string;
  status: "PENDING" | "PUBLISHED" | "REJECTED" | "ARCHIVED";
  listingType: "SALE" | "RENT";
  propertyType: string;
  price: string;
  currency: string;
  createdAt: string;
  reviews: {
    decision: "PUBLISHED" | "REJECTED";
    reason: string | null;
    createdAt: string;
  }[];
};

const statusLabels: Record<Listing["status"], string> = {
  PENDING: "در انتظار تأیید",
  PUBLISHED: "منتشرشده",
  REJECTED: "ردشده",
  ARCHIVED: "بایگانی‌شده",
};

const statusStyles: Record<Listing["status"], string> = {
  PENDING: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800",
  PUBLISHED: "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800",
  REJECTED: "bg-red-100 text-red-800",
  ARCHIVED: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700",
};

export default function MyListingsPage() {
  const [listings, setListings] = useState<Listing[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [needsLogin, setNeedsLogin] = useState(false);

  const loadListings = useCallback(async (signal?: AbortSignal) => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch("/api/my/listings", {
        cache: "no-store",
        signal,
      });

      if (response.status === 401) {
        setNeedsLogin(true);
        setListings([]);
        return;
      }

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error("دریافت آگهی‌ها ناموفق بود.");
      }

      const data: { listings: Listing[] } = await response.json();
      setListings(data.listings);
    } catch (err) {
      if (signal?.aborted) return;
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "خطایی رخ داد.",
      );
    } finally {
      if (!signal?.aborted) setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      void loadListings(controller.signal);
    }, 0);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [loadListings]);

  async function deleteListing(listing: Listing) {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `آگهی «${listing.title}» برای همیشه حذف شود؟`,
    );

    if (!confirmed) return;

    setDeletingId(listing.id);
    setError("");

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        `/api/my/listings/${encodeURIComponent(listing.id)}`,
        { method: "DELETE" },
      );

      if (response.status === 401) {
        setNeedsLogin(true);
        return;
      }

      if (!response.ok) {
        const data = await response.json();
        throw new Error(data.error || "حذف آگهی ناموفق بود.");
      }

      setListings((current) =>
        current.filter((item) => item.id !== listing.id),
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "خطایی رخ داد.",
      );
    } finally {
      setDeletingId(null);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main dir="rtl" className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 px-4 py-10">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
        <div className="mb-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">
              آگهی‌های من
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">
              مدیریت آگهی‌های ثبت‌شده در خانه‌یار
            </p>
          </div>

          <Link
            href="/listings/new"
            className="rounded-lg bg-emerald-700 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-emerald-800"
          >
            + ثبت آگهی جدید
          </Link>
        </div>

        {needsLogin ? (
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-sm">
            <p className="mb-5">برای مشاهده آگهی‌های خود وارد شوید.</p>
            <Link
              href="/login"
              className="rounded-lg bg-emerald-700 px-5 py-3 text-white"
            >
              ورود به حساب
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : loading ? (
          <p className="rounded-xl bg-white p-6 text-slate-600">
            در حال دریافت آگهی‌ها...
          </p>
        ) : (
          <>
            {error && (
              <p role="alert" className="mb-5 rounded-lg bg-red-50 p-4 text-red-700">
                {error}
              </p>
            )}

            {!error && listings.length === 0 && (
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-sm">
                <p>هنوز آگهی‌ای با این حساب ثبت نکرده‌اید.</p>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="space-y-4">
              {listings.map((listing) => (
                <article
                  key={listing.id}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                        {listing.title}
                      </h2>

                      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                        {listing.city} |{" "}
                        {listing.listingType === "SALE" ? "فروش" : "اجاره"}
                      </p>

                      <p className="mt-2 font-semibold text-emerald-800">
                        {Number(listing.price).toLocaleString("fa-IR")}{" "}
                        {listing.currency}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <span
                      className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-sm font-medium ${statusStyles[listing.status]}`}
                    >
                      {statusLabels[listing.status]}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {listing.status === "REJECTED" &&
                    listing.reviews[0]?.decision === "REJECTED" &&
                    listing.reviews[0]?.reason && (
                      <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4">
                        <p className="font-bold text-red-800">
                          دلیل رد آگهی
                        </p>
                        <p className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm text-red-700">
                          {listing.reviews[0].reason}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    )}

                  <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
                    {listing.status === "PUBLISHED" && (
                      <Link
                        href={`/listings/${listing.id}`}
                        className="rounded-lg border border-emerald-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-50"
                      >
                        مشاهده آگهی
                      </Link>
                    )}

                    <Link
                      href={`/dashboard/listings/${listing.id}/edit`}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-blue-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-50"
                    >
                      ویرایش آگهی
                    </Link>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => void deleteListing(listing)}
                      disabled={deletingId !== null}
                      className="rounded-lg border border-red-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      {deletingId === listing.id
                        ? "در حال حذف..."
                        : "حذف آگهی"}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </article>
              ))}
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        <Link
          href="/"
          className="mt-8 inline-block text-sm text-slate-600 hover:text-emerald-700"
        >
          بازگشت به صفحه اصلی
        </Link>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";

export default function NewListingPage() {
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);
    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    try {
      const formData = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
      const response = await fetch("/api/listings", {
        method: "POST",
        body: formData,
      });

      const result = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        setError(
          typeof result.error === "string"
            ? result.error
            : "ثبت آگهی انجام نشد. اطلاعات را بررسی کن.",
        );
        return;
      }

      setMessage(
        `${result.message} کد آگهی: ${result.listing.id}`,
      );
      event.currentTarget.reset();
    } catch {
      setError("ارتباط با سرور برقرار نشد. دوباره تلاش کن.");
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main dir="rtl" className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-900">
      <header className="border-b bg-white">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-4xl items-center justify-between px-5 py-5">
          <Link href="/" className="text-xl font-black text-emerald-700">
            خانه‌یار
          </Link>
          <Link
            href="/"
            className="text-sm text-slate-600 hover:text-emerald-700"
          >
            بازگشت به صفحه اصلی
          </Link>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-5 py-12">
        <div className="mb-8">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-700">
            ثبت آگهی جدید
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black">ملکت را معرفی کن</h1>
          <p className="mt-3 leading-7 text-slate-500">
            اطلاعات ملک را دقیق وارد کن تا متقاضیان بتوانند آن را بهتر پیدا کنند.
          </p>
        </div>

        <form
          onSubmit={handleSubmit}
          className="space-y-8 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm md:p-8"
        >
          <section>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">اطلاعات اصلی</h2>
            <div className="mt-5 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                عنوان آگهی *
                <input
                  name="title"
                  required
                  minLength={5}
                  maxLength={120}
                  placeholder="مثلاً آپارتمان دوخوابه نزدیک مترو"
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 font-normal outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
                />
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                نوع معامله *
                <select
                  name="listingType"
                  required
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 font-normal"
                >
                  <option value="SALE">فروش</option>
                  <option value="RENT">اجاره</option>
                </select>
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                نوع ملک *
                <select
                  name="propertyType"
                  required
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 font-normal"
                >
                  <option value="APARTMENT">آپارتمان</option>
                  <option value="HOUSE">خانه</option>
                  <option value="VILLA">ویلا</option>
                  <option value="LAND">زمین</option>
                  <option value="COMMERCIAL">تجاری</option>
                </select>
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                شهر *
                <input
                  name="city"
                  required
                  minLength={2}
                  maxLength={100}
                  placeholder="مثلاً مسکو"
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 font-normal outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
                />
              </label>
            </div>

            <label className="mt-5 block text-sm font-semibold">
              توضیحات ملک *
              <textarea
                name="description"
                required
                minLength={20}
                maxLength={5000}
                rows={5}
                placeholder="درباره وضعیت ملک، امکانات و موقعیت آن توضیح بده..."
                className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 font-normal outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
              />
            </label>
          </section>

          <section className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-7">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">قیمت و مشخصات</h2>
            <div className="mt-5 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                قیمت *
                <input
                  name="price"
                  type="number"
                  required
                  min="0.01"
                  step="0.01"
                  placeholder="مثلاً 18500000"
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 font-normal"
                />
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                واحد پول *
                <select
                  name="currency"
                  required
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 font-normal"
                >
                  <option value="RUB">روبل روسیه (RUB)</option>
                  <option value="IRR">ریال ایران (IRR)</option>
                  <option value="USD">دلار آمریکا (USD)</option>
                </select>
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                مساحت (متر مربع) *
                <input
                  name="area"
                  type="number"
                  required
                  min="0.01"
                  step="0.01"
                  placeholder="مثلاً 86"
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 font-normal"
                />
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                تعداد اتاق
                <input
                  name="rooms"
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  step="1"
                  placeholder="مثلاً 3"
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 font-normal"
                />
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                طبقه
                <input
                  name="floor"
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  step="1"
                  placeholder="مثلاً 4"
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 font-normal"
                />
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold">
                تعداد کل طبقات ساختمان
                <input
                  name="totalFloors"
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  step="1"
                  placeholder="مثلاً 9"
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 font-normal"
                />
              </label>

              <label className="block text-sm font-semibold md:col-span-2">
                آدرس دقیق (اختیاری)
                <input
                  name="address"
                  maxLength={300}
                  placeholder="خیابان، منطقه یا نشانی ملک"
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 font-normal"
                />
              </label>
            </div>
          </section>

          <div className="rounded-xl bg-amber-50 p-4 text-sm leading-7 text-amber-900">
            فعلاً آگهی به کاربر آزمایشی متصل می‌شود و پس از ثبت، منتظر تأیید مدیر می‌ماند.
            برای استفاده عمومی باید سیستم ورود کاربران پیاده‌سازی شود.
          </div>

          {message && (
            <div
              role="status"
              className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-4 text-sm leading-7 text-emerald-900"
            >
              {message}
            </div>
          )}

          {error && (
            <div
              role="alert"
              className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm leading-7 text-red-800"
            >
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={submitting}
              className="rounded-xl bg-emerald-700 px-6 py-4 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-emerald-800 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {submitting ? "در حال ثبت..." : "ثبت آگهی"}
            </button>
            <Link
              href="/"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-6 py-4 text-center font-bold transition hover:bg-slate-50"
            >
              انصراف
            </Link>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

----- FILE: backups\profile-dashboard-20261018-025556\apps\web\app\profile\page.tsx -----
import Link from "next/link";
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { getCurrentUser } from "../../src/lib/auth";
import ProfileForm from "./ProfileForm";
import styles from "./ProfileForm.module.css";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "پروفایل من | خانه‌یار",
  description: "مشاهده و مدیریت اطلاعات حساب کاربری خانه‌یار",
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};



export default async function ProfilePage() {
  const user = await getCurrentUser();

  if (!user) {
    redirect("/login?next=%2Fprofile");
  }

  return (
    <main className={styles.page} dir="rtl">
      <div className={styles.container}>
        <header className={styles.heading}>
          <div className={styles.breadcrumb}>
            <Link href="/">خانه</Link>
            <span aria-hidden="true">/</span>
            <span>حساب کاربری</span>
          </div>

          <span className={styles.eyebrow}>حساب من</span>
          <h1>پروفایل کاربری</h1>
          <p>
            اطلاعات حساب خود را مشاهده و به‌روز نگه دار.
          </p>
        </header>

        <section className={styles.profileCard}>
          <div className={styles.identity}>
            <div className={styles.avatar} aria-hidden="true">
              {(user.name?.trim().charAt(0) || user.email.charAt(0)).toUpperCase()}
            </div>

            <div className={styles.identityText}>
              <h2>{user.name?.trim() || "کاربر خانه‌یار"}</h2>
              <p>{user.email}</p>
              <span className={styles.role}>
                {user.role === "ADMIN" ? "مدیر سامانه" : "عضو خانه‌یار"}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className={styles.separator} />

          <ProfileForm
            initialName={user.name ?? ""}
            initialPhone={user.phone ?? ""}
            email={user.email}
            role={user.role}
            createdAt={user.createdAt.toISOString()}
          />
        </section>

        <aside className={styles.securityNote}>
          <span aria-hidden="true">◇</span>
          <div>
            <strong>امنیت حساب شما مهم است</strong>
            <p>
              رمز عبور و اطلاعات ورود خود را در اختیار دیگران قرار نده.
            </p>
          </div>
        </aside>

        <footer className={styles.footer}>
          <Link href="/dashboard">پنل کاربری</Link>
          <Link href="/help/account">راهنمای حساب کاربری</Link>
          <Link href="/">بازگشت به خانه</Link>
        </footer>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

----- FILE: backups\profile-fix-20261017-154753\route.ts -----
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { z } from "zod";
import { getCurrentUser } from "../../../../src/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "../../../../src/lib/prisma";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const profileSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100),
  phone: z.string().trim().max(30),
}).strict();

function jsonError(message: string, status: number) {
  return NextResponse.json(
    { message },
    {
      status,
      headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" },
    },
  );
}

function normalizePhone(value: string): string | null {
  const persianDigits = "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹";
  const arabicDigits = "٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩";

  const normalized = value
    .replace(/[۰-۹]/g, (digit) => String(persianDigits.indexOf(digit)))
    .replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (digit) => String(arabicDigits.indexOf(digit)))
    .trim();

  if (!normalized) return null;

  if (!/^[+0-9().\-\s]+$/.test(normalized)) {
    throw new Error("PHONE_FORMAT");
  }

  const digits = normalized.replace(/\D/g, "");

  if (digits.length < 7 || digits.length > 15) {
    throw new Error("PHONE_FORMAT");
  }

  return normalized;
}

export async function PATCH(request: Request) {
  const origin = request.headers.get("origin");

  let expectedOrigin: string;

  try {
    expectedOrigin = new URL(request.url).origin;
  } catch {
    return jsonError("درخواست معتبر نیست.", 400);
  }

  if (!origin || origin !== expectedOrigin) {
    return jsonError("درخواست از مبدأ مجاز ارسال نشده است.", 403);
  }

  const user = await getCurrentUser();

  if (!user) {
    return jsonError("ابتدا وارد حساب خود شوید.", 401);
  }

  let body: unknown;

  try {
    body = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return jsonError("ساختار درخواست معتبر نیست.", 400);
  }

  const parsed = profileSchema.safeParse(body);

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return jsonError("نام یا شماره تلفن معتبر نیست.", 400);
  }

  let phone: string | null;

  try {
    phone = normalizePhone(parsed.data.phone);
  } catch {
    return jsonError(
      "شماره تلفن باید شامل ارقام و نویسه‌های مجاز باشد و ۷ تا ۱۵ رقم داشته باشد.",
      400,
    );
  }

  try {
    if (phone) {
      const existingPhone = await prisma.user.findFirst({
        where: {
          phone,
          NOT: { id: user.id },
        },
        select: { id: true },
      });

      if (existingPhone) {
        return jsonError("این شماره تلفن قبلاً ثبت شده است.", 409);
      }
    }

    const updatedUser = await prisma.user.update({
      where: { id: user.id },
      data: {
        name: parsed.data.name,
        phone,
      },
      select: {
        id: true,
        name: true,
        email: true,
        phone: true,
        role: true,
        createdAt: true,
      },
    });

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        message: "اطلاعات پروفایل ذخیره شد.",
        user: {
          ...updatedUser,
          createdAt: updatedUser.createdAt.toISOString(),
        },
      },
      { headers: { "Cache-Control": "no-store" } },
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const databaseError = error as { code?: string };

    if (databaseError.code === "P2002") {
      return jsonError("این شماره تلفن قبلاً ثبت شده است.", 409);
    }

    console.error("Profile update failed:", error);
    return jsonError("ذخیره اطلاعات با خطا مواجه شد.", 500);
  }
}

----- FILE: backups\repair-20261017-061332\schema.prisma -----
// Real Estate Platform - Database Schema

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

enum UserRole {
  USER
  ADMIN
}

enum ListingType {
  SALE
  RENT
}

enum PropertyType {
  APARTMENT
  HOUSE
  VILLA
  LAND
  COMMERCIAL
}

enum ListingStatus {
  PENDING
  PUBLISHED
  REJECTED
  ARCHIVED
}

model User {
  id           String     @id @default(cuid())
  name         String?
  email        String     @unique
  phone        String?    @unique
  passwordHash String
  role         UserRole   @default(USER)

  listings     Listing[]
  favorites    Favorite[]

  createdAt    DateTime   @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime   @updatedAt
}

model Listing {
  id           String        @id @default(cuid())
  title        String        @db.VarChar(120)
  description  String        @db.Text
  listingType  ListingType
  propertyType PropertyType
  status       ListingStatus @default(PENDING)

  price        Decimal       @db.Decimal(14, 2)
  currency     String        @default("RUB") @db.VarChar(3)
  area         Decimal       @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  rooms        Int?
  floor        Int?
  totalFloors  Int?

  city         String        @db.VarChar(100)
  address      String?       @db.VarChar(300)
  latitude     Float?
  longitude    Float?

  ownerId      String
  owner        User          @relation(fields: [ownerId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  images       ListingImage[]
  favorites    Favorite[]

  createdAt    DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime      @updatedAt

  @@index([status, createdAt])
  @@index([city, status])
  @@index([listingType, propertyType, status])
  @@index([price])
  @@index([ownerId])
}

model ListingImage {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  url       String   @db.VarChar(2048)
  sortOrder Int      @default(0)

  listingId String
  listing   Listing  @relation(fields: [listingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([listingId, sortOrder])
}

model Favorite {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String
  listingId String

  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  listing   Listing  @relation(fields: [listingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@unique([userId, listingId])
  @@index([listingId])
}


enum ReviewDecision {
  PUBLISHED
  REJECTED
}

model ListingReview {
  id         String         @id @default(cuid())
  listingId  String
  reviewerId String?
  decision   ReviewDecision
  reason     String?        @db.VarChar(1000)
  createdAt  DateTime       @default(now())

  listing    Listing        @relation(fields: [listingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  reviewer   User?          @relation(fields: [reviewerId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  @@index([listingId, createdAt])
  @@index([reviewerId])
}

// Real Estate Platform - Database Schema

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

enum UserRole {
  USER
  ADMIN
}

enum ListingType {
  SALE
  RENT
}

enum PropertyType {
  APARTMENT
  HOUSE
  VILLA
  LAND
  COMMERCIAL
}

enum ListingStatus {
  PENDING
  PUBLISHED
  REJECTED
  ARCHIVED
}

model User {
  reviews ListingReview[]
  id           String     @id @default(cuid())
  name         String?
  email        String     @unique
  phone        String?    @unique
  passwordHash String
  role         UserRole   @default(USER)

  listings     Listing[]
  favorites    Favorite[]

  createdAt    DateTime   @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime   @updatedAt
}

model Listing {
  reviews ListingReview[]
  id           String        @id @default(cuid())
  title        String        @db.VarChar(120)
  description  String        @db.Text
  listingType  ListingType
  propertyType PropertyType
  status       ListingStatus @default(PENDING)

  price        Decimal       @db.Decimal(14, 2)
  currency     String        @default("RUB") @db.VarChar(3)
  area         Decimal       @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  rooms        Int?
  floor        Int?
  totalFloors  Int?

  city         String        @db.VarChar(100)
  address      String?       @db.VarChar(300)
  latitude     Float?
  longitude    Float?

  ownerId      String
  owner        User          @relation(fields: [ownerId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  images       ListingImage[]
  favorites    Favorite[]

  createdAt    DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime      @updatedAt

  @@index([status, createdAt])
  @@index([city, status])
  @@index([listingType, propertyType, status])
  @@index([price])
  @@index([ownerId])
}

model ListingImage {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  url       String   @db.VarChar(2048)
  sortOrder Int      @default(0)

  listingId String
  listing   Listing  @relation(fields: [listingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([listingId, sortOrder])
}

model Favorite {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String
  listingId String

  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  listing   Listing  @relation(fields: [listingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@unique([userId, listingId])
  @@index([listingId])
}


enum ReviewDecision {
  PUBLISHED
  REJECTED
}

model ListingReview {
  id         String         @id @default(cuid())
  listingId  String
  reviewerId String?
  decision   ReviewDecision
  reason     String?        @db.VarChar(1000)
  createdAt  DateTime       @default(now())

  listing    Listing        @relation(fields: [listingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  reviewer   User?          @relation(fields: [reviewerId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  @@index([listingId, createdAt])
  @@index([reviewerId])
}

// Real Estate Platform - Database Schema

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

  provider = "postgresql"

}

enum UserRole {
  USER
  ADMIN
}

enum ListingType {
  SALE
  RENT
}

enum PropertyType {
  APARTMENT
  HOUSE
  VILLA
  LAND
  COMMERCIAL
}

enum ListingStatus {
  PENDING
  PUBLISHED
  REJECTED
  ARCHIVED
}

model User {
  id           String     @id @default(cuid())
  name         String?
  email        String     @unique
  phone        String?    @unique
  passwordHash String
  role         UserRole   @default(USER)

  listings     Listing[]
  favorites    Favorite[]

  createdAt    DateTime   @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime   @updatedAt
}

model Listing {
  id           String        @id @default(cuid())
  title        String        @db.VarChar(120)
  description  String        @db.Text
  listingType  ListingType
  propertyType PropertyType
  status       ListingStatus @default(PENDING)

  price        Decimal       @db.Decimal(14, 2)
  currency     String        @default("RUB") @db.VarChar(3)
  area         Decimal       @db.Decimal(10, 2)
  rooms        Int?
  floor        Int?
  totalFloors  Int?

  city         String        @db.VarChar(100)
  address      String?       @db.VarChar(300)
  latitude     Float?
  longitude    Float?

  ownerId      String
  owner        User          @relation(fields: [ownerId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  images       ListingImage[]
  favorites    Favorite[]

  createdAt    DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime      @updatedAt

  @@index([status, createdAt])
  @@index([city, status])
  @@index([listingType, propertyType, status])
  @@index([price])
  @@index([ownerId])
}

model ListingImage {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  url       String   @db.VarChar(2048)
  sortOrder Int      @default(0)

  listingId String
  listing   Listing  @relation(fields: [listingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([listingId, sortOrder])
}

model Favorite {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  userId    String
  listingId String

  user      User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  listing   Listing  @relation(fields: [listingId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@unique([userId, listingId])
  @@index([listingId])

----- FILE: backups\shared-navigation-20261018-015018\apps\web\app\listings\[id]\page.tsx -----
import SiteNavigation from "../../_components/SiteNavigation";
import UnreadMessagesLink from "../../_components/UnreadMessagesLink";
import InquiryForm from "./InquiryForm";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { prisma } from "@/src/lib/prisma";
import PropertyGallery, { ShareListing } from "./PropertyGallery";
import FavoriteButton from "../../_components/FavoriteButton";
import "./detail.css";

export const instant = false;

type Props = {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

const propertyNames: Record<string, string> = {
  APARTMENT: "آپارتمان",
  HOUSE: "خانه",
  VILLA: "ویلا",
  LAND: "زمین",
  COMMERCIAL: "ملک تجاری",
};

const currencyNames: Record<string, string> = {
  RUB: "روبل",
  IRR: "ریال",
  IRT: "تومان",
  USD: "دلار",
  EUR: "یورو",
};

function formatNumber(value: string, digits = 0) {
  const parsed = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(parsed)
    ? new Intl.NumberFormat("fa-IR", {
        maximumFractionDigits: digits,
      }).format(parsed)
    : value;
}

function imageIsSafe(url: string) {
  if (url.startsWith("/") && !url.startsWith("//")) {
    return true;
  }

  try {
    const parsed = new URL(url);
    return parsed.protocol === "https:" || parsed.protocol === "http:";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export default async function ListingDetailPage({ params }: Props) {
  const { id } = await params;

  const listing = await prisma.listing.findFirst({
    where: {
      id,
      status: "PUBLISHED",
    },
    select: {
      id: true,
      title: true,
      description: true,
      listingType: true,
      propertyType: true,
      price: true,
      currency: true,
      area: true,
      rooms: true,
      floor: true,
      totalFloors: true,
      city: true,
      address: true,
      createdAt: true,
      images: {
        orderBy: [{ sortOrder: "asc" }, { createdAt: "asc" }],
        take: 20,
        select: { url: true },
      },
    },
  });

  if (!listing) {
    notFound();
  }

  const pictures = listing.images
    .map((item) => item.url)
    .filter(imageIsSafe);

  const isRent = listing.listingType === "RENT";
  const propertyName = propertyNames[listing.propertyType] ?? "ملک";
  const price = formatNumber(listing.price.toString());
  const area = formatNumber(listing.area.toString(), 2);
  const currency = currencyNames[listing.currency] ?? listing.currency;
  const publishedDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fa-IR", {
    dateStyle: "long",
  }).format(listing.createdAt);

  const facts: { label: string; value: string }[] = [
    { label: "نوع ملک", value: propertyName },
    { label: "نوع معامله", value: isRent ? "اجاره" : "فروش" },
    { label: "متراژ", value: `${area} متر مربع` },
    { label: "شهر", value: listing.city },
  ];

  if (listing.rooms !== null) {
    facts.push({
      label: "تعداد اتاق",
      value: listing.rooms.toLocaleString("fa-IR"),
    });
  }

  if (listing.floor !== null) {
    facts.push({
      label: "طبقه",
      value: listing.floor.toLocaleString("fa-IR"),
    });
  }

  if (listing.totalFloors !== null) {
    facts.push({
      label: "تعداد طبقات ساختمان",
      value: listing.totalFloors.toLocaleString("fa-IR"),
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="hd-page" dir="rtl">
      <SiteNavigation />
      <header className="hd-header">
        <div className="hd-container hd-header-inner">
          <Link href="/" className="hd-brand">
            <span className="hd-brand-icon" aria-hidden="true">⌂</span>
            <strong>خانه‌یار</strong>
          </Link>

          <nav className="hd-nav" aria-label="ناوبری">
            <Link href="/search">جست‌وجوی ملک</Link>
            <Link href="/dashboard/listings">آگهی‌های من</Link>
            <UnreadMessagesLink />
            <Link href="/listings/new" className="hd-create-link">

import SiteNavigation from "../_components/SiteNavigation";
import UnreadMessagesLink from "../_components/UnreadMessagesLink";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Suspense } from "react";
import type { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";
import { prisma } from "@/src/lib/prisma";
import PropertyCard from "../_components/PropertyCard";
import "../market.css";

type RawValue = string | string[] | undefined;

type SearchParams = {
  city?: RawValue;
  deal?: RawValue;
  property?: RawValue;
  minPrice?: RawValue;
  maxPrice?: RawValue;
  minArea?: RawValue;
  maxArea?: RawValue;
  rooms?: RawValue;
  sort?: RawValue;
  page?: RawValue;
};

type SearchFilters = {
  city: string;
  deal: "SALE" | "RENT" | "";
  property: "APARTMENT" | "HOUSE" | "VILLA" | "LAND" | "COMMERCIAL" | "";
  minPrice: string;
  maxPrice: string;
  minArea: string;
  maxArea: string;
  rooms: string;
  sort: "newest" | "price_asc" | "price_desc" | "area_desc";
};

const PAGE_SIZE = 12;

const propertyTypes = [
  "APARTMENT",
  "HOUSE",
  "VILLA",
  "LAND",
  "COMMERCIAL",
] as const;

const sortOptions = [
  "newest",
  "price_asc",
  "price_desc",
  "area_desc",
] as const;

function single(value: RawValue): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

function moneyInput(value: RawValue): string {
  const raw = single(value);
  if (!/^\d{1,12}$/.test(raw)) return "";
  return String(Number(raw));
}

function areaInput(value: RawValue): string {
  const raw = single(value);
  if (!/^\d{1,8}$/.test(raw)) return "";
  return String(Number(raw));
}

function roomInput(value: RawValue): string {
  const raw = single(value);
  if (!/^\d{1,2}$/.test(raw)) return "";
  const number = Number(raw);
  return number <= 30 ? String(number) : "";
}

function parsePage(value: RawValue): number {
  const raw = single(value);

  if (!/^[1-9]\d{0,6}$/.test(raw)) {
    return 1;
  }

  return Number(raw);
}

function parseFilters(params: SearchParams): SearchFilters {
  const dealValue = single(params.deal);
  const propertyValue = single(params.property);
  const sortValue = single(params.sort);

  const deal =
    dealValue === "SALE" || dealValue === "RENT"
      ? dealValue
      : "";

  const property =
    propertyTypes.find((item) => item === propertyValue) ?? "";

  const sort =
    sortOptions.find((item) => item === sortValue) ?? "newest";

  return {
    city: single(params.city).slice(0, 100),
    deal,
    property,
    minPrice: moneyInput(params.minPrice),
    maxPrice: moneyInput(params.maxPrice),
    minArea: areaInput(params.minArea),
    maxArea: areaInput(params.maxArea),
    rooms: roomInput(params.rooms),
    sort,
  };
}

function buildWhere(filters: SearchFilters): Prisma.ListingWhereInput {
  const where: Prisma.ListingWhereInput = {
    status: "PUBLISHED",
  };

  if (filters.city) {
    where.city = {
      contains: filters.city,
      mode: "insensitive",
    };
  }

  if (filters.deal) {
    where.listingType = filters.deal;
  }

  if (filters.property) {
    where.propertyType = filters.property;
  }

  if (filters.minPrice || filters.maxPrice) {
    where.price = {
      ...(filters.minPrice ? { gte: filters.minPrice } : {}),
      ...(filters.maxPrice ? { lte: filters.maxPrice } : {}),
    };
  }

  if (filters.minArea || filters.maxArea) {
    where.area = {
      ...(filters.minArea ? { gte: filters.minArea } : {}),
      ...(filters.maxArea ? { lte: filters.maxArea } : {}),
    };
  }

  if (filters.rooms) {
    where.rooms = { gte: Number(filters.rooms) };
  }

  return where;
}

function sortBy(value: SearchFilters["sort"]): Prisma.ListingOrderByWithRelationInput[] {
  switch (value) {
    case "price_asc":
      return [{ price: "asc" }, { id: "desc" }];
    case "price_desc":
      return [{ price: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }];
    case "area_desc":
      return [{ area: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }];
    default:
      return [{ createdAt: "desc" }, { id: "desc" }];
  }
}

function pageUrl(filters: SearchFilters, page: number): string {
  const query = new URLSearchParams();

  const values: Record<string, string> = {
    city: filters.city,
    deal: filters.deal,
    property: filters.property,
    minPrice: filters.minPrice,
    maxPrice: filters.maxPrice,
    minArea: filters.minArea,
    maxArea: filters.maxArea,
    rooms: filters.rooms,
    sort: filters.sort === "newest" ? "" : filters.sort,
  };

  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(values)) {
    if (value !== "") {
      query.set(key, value);
    }
  }

  if (page > 1) {
    query.set("page", String(page));
  }

  const encoded = query.toString();
  return encoded ? `/search?${encoded}` : "/search";
}

async function SearchResults({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<SearchParams>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const filters = parseFilters(params);
  const where = buildWhere(filters);

  const requestedPage = parsePage(params.page);
  const total = await prisma.listing.count({ where });

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PAGE_SIZE));
  const currentPage = Math.min(requestedPage, totalPages);

  const listings = await prisma.listing.findMany({
    where,
    orderBy: sortBy(filters.sort),
    skip: (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE,
    take: PAGE_SIZE,
    select: {
      id: true,
      title: true,
      city: true,
      area: true,
      price: true,
      currency: true,
      rooms: true,
      propertyType: true,
      listingType: true,
      images: {
        take: 1,
        orderBy: [{ sortOrder: "asc" }, { id: "asc" }],
        select: { url: true },
      },
    },
  });

  const firstResult =
    total === 0 ? 0 : (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE + 1;

  const lastResult =
    total === 0 ? 0 : Math.min(currentPage * PAGE_SIZE, total);

  return (
    <div className="hy-advanced-layout">
      <aside className="hy-advanced-sidebar">
        <div className="hy-filter-header">
          <h2>فیلترهای جست‌وجو</h2>
          <Link href="/search">پاک کردن فیلترها</Link>
        </div>

        <form action="/search" method="get" className="hy-filter-form">
          <label className="hy-field">
            <span>شهر</span>
            <input
              name="city"
              type="search"
              defaultValue={filters.city}
              maxLength={100}
              placeholder="مثلاً مسکو"
            />
          </label>

          <label className="hy-field">
            <span>نوع معامله</span>
            <select name="deal" defaultValue={filters.deal}>
              <option value="">خرید و اجاره</option>
              <option value="SALE">خرید</option>
              <option value="RENT">اجاره</option>
            </select>
          </label>

          <label className="hy-field">
            <span>نوع ملک</span>
            <select name="property" defaultValue={filters.property}>
              <option value="">همه املاک</option>
              <option value="APARTMENT">آپارتمان</option>
              <option value="HOUSE">خانه</option>
              <option value="VILLA">ویلا</option>
              <option value="LAND">زمین</option>
              <option value="COMMERCIAL">تجاری</option>
            </select>
          </label>

          <fieldset className="hy-filter-group">
            <legend>محدوده قیمت</legend>
            <div className="hy-filter-pair">
              <label className="hy-field">
                <span>از</span>
                <input
                  name="minPrice"
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  max="999999999999"
                  step="1"
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  defaultValue={filters.minPrice}
                  placeholder="حداقل"
                />
              </label>

              <label className="hy-field">
                <span>تا</span>
                <input
                  name="maxPrice"
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  max="999999999999"
                  step="1"
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  defaultValue={filters.maxPrice}
                  placeholder="حداکثر"
                />
              </label>
            </div>
            <p className="hy-filter-hint">
              مبلغ را با واحد پول درج‌شده در آگهی وارد کنید.
              آگهی‌ها ممکن است ارزهای متفاوتی داشته باشند.
            </p>
          </fieldset>

          <fieldset className="hy-filter-group">
            <legend>متراژ ملک (متر مربع)</legend>
            <div className="hy-filter-pair">
              <label className="hy-field">
                <span>از</span>
                <input
                  name="minArea"
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  max="99999999"
                  step="1"
                  defaultValue={filters.minArea}
                  placeholder="حداقل"
                />
              </label>

              <label className="hy-field">
                <span>تا</span>
                <input
                  name="maxArea"
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  max="99999999"
                  step="1"
                  defaultValue={filters.maxArea}
                  placeholder="حداکثر"
                />
              </label>
            </div>
          </fieldset>

          <label className="hy-field">
            <span>حداقل تعداد اتاق</span>
            <select name="rooms" defaultValue={filters.rooms}>
              <option value="">فرقی ندارد</option>
              <option value="1">۱ اتاق</option>
              <option value="2">۲ اتاق</option>
              <option value="3">۳ اتاق</option>
              <option value="4">۴ اتاق</option>
              <option value="5">۵ اتاق یا بیشتر</option>
            </select>
          </label>

          <label className="hy-field">
            <span>مرتب‌سازی</span>
            <select name="sort" defaultValue={filters.sort}>
              <option value="newest">جدیدترین آگهی‌ها</option>
              <option value="price_asc">ارزان‌ترین</option>
              <option value="price_desc">گران‌ترین</option>
              <option value="area_desc">بیشترین متراژ</option>
            </select>
          </label>

          <button
            type="submit"
            className="hy-button hy-button-primary hy-filter-submit"
          >
            نمایش نتایج
          </button>
        </form>
      </aside>

      <section className="hy-advanced-results" aria-label="نتایج جست‌وجو">
        <div className="hy-advanced-summary">
          <div>
            <h2>آگهی‌های املاک</h2>
            <p>
              {total.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} آگهی مطابق فیلترها
            </p>
          </div>
          <span className="hy-results-range">
            نمایش {firstResult.toLocaleString("fa-IR")} تا{" "}
            {lastResult.toLocaleString("fa-IR")}
          </span>
        </div>

        {listings.length ? (
          <div className="hy-advanced-property-grid">
            {listings.map((listing) => (
              <PropertyCard key={listing.id} listing={listing} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="hy-empty">
            <span aria-hidden="true">⌕</span>
            <h3>آگهی مناسبی پیدا نشد</h3>
            <p>محدوده قیمت، شهر یا دیگر فیلترها را تغییر بده.</p>
            <Link
              href="/search"
              className="hy-button hy-button-primary"
            >
              نمایش همه آگهی‌ها
            </Link>
          </div>
        )}

        {totalPages > 1 && (
          <nav
            className="hy-pagination"

          >
            {currentPage > 1 ? (
              <Link href={pageUrl(filters, currentPage - 1)}>
                صفحه قبلی
              </Link>
            ) : (
              <span className="hy-page-disabled">صفحه قبلی</span>
            )}

              {totalPages.toLocaleString("fa-IR")}

              </Link>
            ) : (
              <span className="hy-page-disabled">صفحه بعدی</span>
            )}
          </nav>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
